[170] assistant: 我查了本机 skills，没有发现明确叫 `PM` / 产品经理的本地 skill。这里按最接近的本地 `documents` 商业简报结构 + `stripe-best-practices` 支付方案结构，给你一版文字版“内部支付方案”。

**内部支付方案**
目标：让 2D Godot 游戏支持“游戏内点击购买完整版，玩家用加密货币付款，付款确认后自动解锁内容”。

核心原则：
- 游戏客户端不直接处理钱包私钥、链上交易和支付验证。
- 支付发生在外部 Web Checkout 页面。
- 后端是唯一可信来源，负责订单、付款确认、授权解锁。
- Godot 只负责打开支付页、轮询订单状态、保存本地解锁状态。

**用户流程**
```text
玩家打开 Demo
↓
点击 Unlock Full Version
↓
游戏向后端创建订单
↓
后端返回 checkout_url + order_id
↓
游戏打开浏览器支付页
↓
玩家连接钱包并支付
↓
支付网关 webhook 或链上监听确认付款
↓
后端把订单标记为 paid
↓
Godot 轮询到 paid
↓
自动解锁完整版
```

**MVP 功能范围**
第一版只做一个商品：

```text
Product ID: full_game
Name: Full Version
Price: $4.99 或 $9.99
Network: Polygon / BSC 优先
Currency: USDT / USDC / MATIC
Unlock: 全章节 / 完整内容 / 高级收藏系统
```

暂时不做：
- NFT 道具
- 游戏内交易市场
- Play-to-earn
- 钱包登录账号体系
- 多商品商城
- 退款自动化
- 复杂订阅

**系统模块**
```text
Godot Client
- 显示购买入口
- 获取 device_id
- 创建订单
- 打开 checkout_url
- 轮询订单状态
- paid 后解锁内容
- 本地缓存 license

Backend API
- 创建订单
- 查询订单状态
- 接收 webhook
- 验证支付金额/币种/订单号
- 生成 license
- 返回解锁状态

Checkout Page
- 显示商品信息
- 连接钱包
- 发起支付
- 显示支付成功/等待确认

Payment Provider / Chain Listener
- NOWPayments / Coinbase Commerce / 自建链上监听
- 通知后端付款成功
```

**后端接口**
```text
POST /api/orders
输入: device_id, product_id
输出: order_id, checkout_url

GET /api/orders/:order_id
输出: pending | paid | expired | failed

POST /api/payment-webhook
支付成功回调，只允许支付服务调用

GET /api/license/:device_id
查询该设备是否已解锁
```

**订单状态**
```text
pending   已创建，等待付款
paid      已确认付款，可以解锁
expired   超时未付款
failed    付款失败或金额不匹配
refunded  后续版本再支持
```

**安全要求**
- Godot 客户端不能自己声明“已付款”。
- webhook 必须校验签名。
- 后端必须校验金额、币种、订单 ID、收款地址。
- 每个订单只能解锁一次。
- device_id 只用于低成本授权，不要当作强身份。
- 本地 license 可以被破解，所以高价值内容应以后端状态为准。

**推荐第一版技术选型**
```text
Game: Godot
Backend: Node.js / Express
Database: SQLite 起步，后续 PostgreSQL
Payment: NOWPayments 或 Coinbase Commerce
Network: Polygon / BSC
Currency: USDT / USDC
Hosting: Cloudflare Workers / Render / Fly.io / Railway
```

**为什么第一版不用 UAccess 自动解锁**
UAccess 更适合“付款后下载文件”，不适合直接把支付成功状态推给 Godot。它可以作为手动 license code 方案，但如果要自动解锁，最好用自己的 checkout + 后端订单系统。

**上线顺序**
1. 做 Godot 内 `Unlock Full Version` 按钮。
2. 后端实现订单表和 `POST /api/orders`。
3. 接入一个支付网关 checkout。
4. 实现 webhook，把订单改成 `paid`。
5. Godot 每 3 秒查询订单状态。
6. paid 后写入本地 license 并解锁。
7. 上线前做小额真实支付测试。

**产品判断**
这套方案适合：
- itch.io 免费 Demo
- 自己网站/checkout 收加密货币
- PC 完整版解锁
- 小额一次性购买

不适合：
- Steam 版本
- App Store / Google Play 内购替代
- 强监管地区的大规模商业发行
- 高投机 NFT/GameFi 玩法

建议第一版目标很窄：**只做一次性购买完整版自动解锁**。等这条链路跑通，再考虑钱包账号、NFT 收藏品或多商品商城。

[171] user: 产品内部如何完成支付


[172] assistant: 产品内部完成支付，最稳的是 **“内置购买入口 + 外部 Checkout + 后端确认 + 自动解锁”**。游戏里看起来像内购，但真正付款在网页完成。

**产品内流程**
```text
1. 玩家在 Demo 里看到锁定内容
2. 点击 Unlock Full Version
3. 游戏弹出购买页说明
4. 玩家点 Pay with Crypto
5. 游戏打开浏览器 Checkout
6. 玩家用钱包支付
7. 回到游戏等待确认
8. 游戏自动显示 “Unlocked”
9. 完整版内容开放
```

**游戏内页面设计**
```text
Unlock Full Version

Includes:
- All chapters
- Full treasure collection
- Companion rewards
- Challenge levels

Price: $4.99
Pay with crypto

[Unlock Now]
[Restore Purchase]
```

**关键按钮**
- `Unlock Now`：创建订单并打开支付页
- `I have paid`：手动刷新订单状态
- `Restore Purchase`：根据设备 ID / license code / 邮箱恢复购买
- `Enter License Code`：备用方案，防止自动解锁失败

**内部状态**
```text
free_demo
payment_pending
paid_confirming
unlocked
payment_failed
restore_needed
```

**用户体验细节**
支付后不要让玩家猜：

```text
Waiting for payment confirmation...
This usually takes 10-60 seconds.
You can safely return to the game after payment.
```

如果没确认：

```text
Payment not detected yet.
[Check Again]
[Open Payment Page]
[Enter License Code]
```

**产品规则**
- Demo 可玩，不强制钱包登录。
- 购买只解锁完整版，不影响基础体验。
- 不卖战力，不做抽卡，不做代币奖励。
- 价格用美元展示，支付页再显示链和币种。
- 默认推荐 Polygon / BSC，避免 ETH 主网高 gas。

**最小 MVP**
```text
商品：Full Version
价格：$4.99
支付方式：USDT / USDC / MATIC
解锁方式：后端确认 paid 后自动解锁
备用方式：license code 手动输入
```

**真正完成支付的系统链路**
```text
Godot 创建订单
↓
后端生成 checkout_url
↓
玩家浏览器付款
↓
支付服务 webhook 通知后端
↓
后端标记订单 paid
↓
Godot 查询到 paid
↓
本地保存 unlocked = true
```

一句话：  
**游戏内不要直接收钱；游戏内只发起购买、显示状态、解锁内容。支付和验证交给网页 + 后端。**

[173] user: 如果pc版本可以选择钱包登陆，支付解锁用合约方式可以完成吗


[174] assistant: 可以完成，而且 PC 版比移动端更适合做这套：**钱包登录 + 合约支付 + 自动解锁**。

但建议你把它设计成“可选钱包登录”，不要强制玩家一进游戏就连接钱包。

**推荐产品流程**
```text
玩家打开 PC Demo
↓
可直接试玩
↓
点击 Unlock Full Version
↓
选择 Connect Wallet
↓
浏览器打开钱包连接页
↓
玩家用 MetaMask / WalletConnect 登录
↓
调用合约 pay(order_id)
↓
链上交易确认
↓
后端监听合约事件
↓
Godot 查询订单 paid
↓
自动解锁完整版
```

**核心结构**
```text
Godot PC Client
- 显示登录/购买入口
- 打开钱包网页
- 轮询订单状态
- 解锁内容

Wallet Checkout Web Page
- 连接 MetaMask / WalletConnect
- 显示价格
- 调用智能合约支付
- 显示交易状态

Payment Smart Contract
- 接收付款
- 记录 order_id / buyer / amount
- emit PaymentReceived

Backend Listener
- 监听 PaymentReceived 事件
- 校验金额和订单
- 标记 paid
- 生成 license

Godot Unlock
- 查询 paid
- 保存 unlocked
```

**合约支付逻辑**
合约只做简单收款，不要第一版就做复杂 NFT：

```solidity
function pay(bytes32 orderId) external payable {
    require(msg.value == PRICE, "wrong amount");
    require(!paidOrders[orderId], "already paid");

    paidOrders[orderId] = true;

    emit PaymentReceived(orderId, msg.sender, msg.value);
}
```

如果用 USDC / USDT：

```solidity
function pay(bytes32 orderId) external {
    require(!paidOrders[orderId], "already paid");

    token.transferFrom(msg.sender, treasury, PRICE);

    paidOrders[orderId] = true;

    emit PaymentReceived(orderId, msg.sender, PRICE);
}
```

**为什么仍然需要后端**
即使链上有记录，Godot 客户端不应该自己决定已付款。原因：

- 客户端容易被改
- 需要防止错误金额
- 需要处理链重组/失败交易
- 需要做 license 恢复
- 需要给非技术玩家更稳定体验

后端监听合约事件后，把订单状态变成 `paid`，Godot 只相信后端。

**钱包登录怎么做**
推荐用网页完成，不要直接在 Godot 里接钱包 SDK：

```text
Godot 打开:
https://yourgame.com/checkout?order_id=xxx

网页里:
- Connect Wallet
- Sign message 登录
- Pay with USDC
- Done
```

Godot 只拿：
```text
order_id
wallet_address
status: pending / paid
```

**推荐链**
第一版建议：

```text
Polygon + USDC
或
Base + USDC
或
BSC + USDT
```

不建议第一版用 Ethereum 主网，gas 对小额游戏太贵。

**产品上怎么做最好**
```text
Unlock Full Version
$4.99

Options:
[Pay with Wallet]
[Enter License Code]
[Restore Purchase]
```

支付页显示：
```text
Connect Wallet
Pay 4.99 USDC on Polygon
Waiting for confirmation...
Payment confirmed. You can return to the game.
```

**是否可以不用后端，纯合约解锁？**
技术上可以，但不建议。

纯客户端读取合约：
```text
Godot 查询链上 order_id paid
↓
解锁
```

问题是：
- RPC 不稳定
- 多链处理麻烦
- 客户端容易被篡改
- 恢复购买和客服困难
- 不能方便地封禁异常订单
- 离线授权体验差

所以生产方案还是：

```text
合约 = 收款和公开凭证
后端 = 授权和订单真相
Godot = 展示和解锁
```

**最小可行版本**
1. PC Demo 内加 `Unlock Full Version`
2. 后端创建 `order_id`
3. 网页连接钱包
4. Polygon/Base USDC 合约收款
5. 合约 emit `PaymentReceived`
6. 后端监听事件
7. Godot 轮询订单状态
8. paid 后解锁完整版

这套可以完成，并且比 UAccess 更产品化。

# Gmail

## Overview

Use this skill to turn noisy email threads into clear summaries, action lists, and ready-to-send drafts. Prefer Gmail-native search and read workflows, preserve message context, and avoid changing message state without explicit user intent.

## Preferred Deliverables

- Thread briefs that capture the latest status, decisions, open questions, and next actions.
- Reply or forward drafts that are ready to paste, review, or send.
- Inbox triage lists that group messages by urgency or follow-up state.

## Workflow Skills

| Workflow | Skill |
| --- | --- |
| Inbox triage, urgency ranking, and follow-up detection | [../gmail-inbox-triage/SKILL.md](../gmail-inbox-triage/SKILL.md) |

## Reference Notes

| Task | Reference |
| --- | --- |
| Search planning, refinement, pagination, and body-fetch strategy | [references/search-workflow.md](./references/search-workflow.md) |
| Label application, relabeling, and label-based cleanup | [references/label-actions.md](./references/label-actions.md) |
| Reply drafting, reply-all decisions, and tone matching | [references/reply-workflow.md](./references/reply-workflow.md) |
| Email forwarding, context notes, and intent framing | [references/forward-workflow.md](./references/forward-workflow.md) |

## Mailbox Analysis Pattern

For mailbox analysis requests such as triage, follow-up detection, topic summaries, cleanup, thread understandi<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="607" />rief confidence note when necessary.

## Output Conventions

- Summaries should lead with the latest status, then list decisions, open questions, and action items.
- Inbox triage should use explicit buckets such as urgent, waiting, and FYI when that helps the user scan quickly.
- When ranking urgency or follow-up state, state the search scope and coverage, such as "from the most recent 15 inbox messages" or "from unread inbox messages matching this query."
- When the task depends on whether the user "opened" or ignored email, treat that as an inference from Gmail read state unless the connector exposes stronger engagement data.
- Avoid absolute claims like "the only urgent email" unless the mailbox scan was comprehensive enough to support that conclusion.
- When the result comes from a narrowed search or shortlist, report that confidence and mention what was excluded.
- Draft replies should be concise and ready to paste or send, with greeting, body, and closing when appropriate.
- If a reply depends on missing facts, present a short draft plus a list of unresolved details.
- When multiple emails are involved, reference the sender and timestamp of the message that matters most.
- Avoid repetitive meta-explanations about inaccessible internal sources in normal deliverables. If the user wants provenance, summarize the evidence used; otherwise keep the output focused on the draft, summary, or next action.

## Example Requests

- "Summarize the latest thread with Acme and tell me what I still owe them."
- "Draft a reply that confirms Tuesday works and asks for the final agenda."
- "Go through my unread inbox and group emails into urgent, waiting, and low priority."
- "Prepare a polite follow-up to the recruiter thread if I have not replied yet."

## Light Fallback

If thread or inbox data is missing, say that Gmail access may be unavailable or scoped to the wrong account and ask the user to reconnect or clarify which mailbox or thread should be used.

[161] assistant: 可以做到“支付后直接解锁”，但 **UAccess 本身不太适合直接通知 Godot 游戏自动解锁**。它更适合“付款后下载文件/解锁网页内容”。

要实现自动解锁，推荐三种方案：

**方案 A：半自动，最简单**
```text
游戏内点击 Unlock
↓
打开 UAccess 支付页
↓
玩家付款
↓
UAccess 显示下载页 / 说明页
↓
玩家复制 license code
↓
回到游戏输入 code 解锁
```

优点：最容易做。  
缺点：不是完全自动。

**方案 B：自动解锁，需要你自己的后端**
```text
游戏生成 device_id / order_id
↓
打开你的支付页：?order_id=xxx
↓
玩家用钱包付款
↓
后端监听支付成功
↓
Godot 每几秒查询 /api/order_status
↓
状态变 paid
↓
游戏自动解锁
```

这是最稳的“直接解锁”方案。

**方案 C：钱包签名解锁，偏 Web3**
```text
玩家连接钱包付款
↓
游戏记录钱包地址
↓
后端验证链上交易
↓
钱包地址获得 license
↓
游戏查询 license 状态
```

适合以后做 NFT/钱包身份，但第一版没必要。

**结论：**
- 如果只用 UAccess：更现实的是 **付款后给 license code，玩家手动输入**。
- 如果要“支付后游戏自动解锁”：需要 **自建支付页 + 后端订单系统 + Godot 查询接口**。
- 不建议让 Godot 直接处理链上交易验证，维护成本高。

第一版我建议：
```text
UAccess 购买完整版
↓
购买后展示 license code
↓
玩家输入 code 解锁
```

第二版再升级为：
```text
自建 checkout
↓
Webhook / 链上监听
↓
Godot 自动解锁
```

[162] user: 自建 checkout
↓
Webhook / 链上监听
↓
Godot 自动解锁如何实现


[163] assistant: 实现核心是：**Godot 不直接碰钱包和链上验证，只负责创建订单、打开支付页、查询订单状态。**

**架构**
```text
Godot 客户端
  ↓ create_order
你的后端 API
  ↓ 返回 checkout_url + order_id
浏览器支付页
  ↓ 玩家钱包支付
支付网关 / 链上监听
  ↓ webhook 或确认交易
你的后端更新订单 paid
  ↓ Godot 轮询 order_status
Godot 自动解锁完整版
```

**推荐第一版用支付网关**
比自己监听链简单。可选：
- NOWPayments
- Coinbase Commerce
- Crypto.com Pay
- Stripe crypto 若地区支持
- 自建钱包收款 + 后端监听链，复杂度更高

**后端需要 4 个接口**
```text
POST /api/orders
创建订单，返回 order_id 和 checkout_url

GET /api/orders/:id
查询订单状态：pending / paid / expired

POST /api/payment-webhook
支付网关通知付款成功

GET /api/license/:device_id
查询设备是否已解锁
```

**订单表**
```text
orders
- id
- device_id
- product_id
- amount_usd
- currency
- status: pending | paid | expired | failed
- payment_provider
- payment_id
- wallet_address
- created_at
- paid_at
```

**Godot 流程**
```gdscript
func unlock_full_version():
    var device_id = get_or_create_device_id()
    var res = await api_create_order(device_id)
    OS.shell_open(res.checkout_url)
    start_polling_order(res.order_id)

func start_polling_order(order_id):
    while true:
        await get_tree().create_timer(3.0).timeout
        var status = await api_get_order_status(order_id)
        if status == "paid":
            save_license_locally()
            unlock_content()
            break
```

**后端流程**
```js
app.post("/api/orders", async (req, res) => {
  const order = await db.createOrder({
    device_id: req.body.device_id,
    product_id: "full_game",
    amount_usd: 4.99,
    status: "pending"
  })

  const checkout = await paymentProvider.createCheckout({
    order_id: order.id,
    amount: 4.99,
    currency: "USD",
    success_url: `https://yourgame.com/success?order_id=${order.id}`,
    cancel_url: `https://yourgame.com/cancel?order_id=${order.id}`
  })

  res.json({ order_id: order.id, checkout_url: checkout.url })
})
```

**Webhook**
```js
app.post("/api/payment-webhook", async (req, res) => {
  const event = verifyWebhook(req)

  if (event.type === "payment_confirmed") {
    await db.markPaid(event.order_id)
  }

  res.sendStatus(200)
})
```

**关键安全点**
- 不要只相信 Godot 客户端说“我付了”。
- 必须由支付网关 webhook 或后端链上确认来改 `paid`。
- Godot 本地 license 只能做缓存，真正状态以后端为准。
- 每个订单要有唯一 `order_id`。
- webhook 必须校验签名。
- 支付金额、币种、收款地址必须在后端验证。

**最小可行版本**
1. Node.js 后端
2. SQLite 数据库
3. NOWPayments checkout
4. Godot 打开 checkout URL
5. Godot 每 3 秒轮询订单
6. webhook 收到付款成功后自动解锁

这条路线比直接接 MetaMask / WalletConnect 进 Godot 稳很多。

[170] assistant: 我查了本机 skills，没有发现明确叫 `PM` / 产品经理的本地 skill。这里按最接近的本地 `documents` 商业简报结构 + `stripe-best-practices` 支付方案结构，给你一版文字版“内部支付方案”。

**内部支付方案**
目标：让 2D Godot 游戏支持“游戏内点击购买完整版，玩家用加密货币付款，付款确认后自动解锁内容”。

核心原则：
- 游戏客户端不直接处理钱包私钥、链上交易和支付验证。
- 支付发生在外部 Web Checkout 页面。
- 后端是唯一可信来源，负责订单、付款确认、授权解锁。
- Godot 只负责打开支付页、轮询订单状态、保存本地解锁状态。

**用户流程**
```text
玩家打开 Demo
↓
点击 Unlock Full Version
↓
游戏向后端创建订单
↓
后端返回 checkout_url + order_id
↓
游戏打开浏览器支付页
↓
玩家连接钱包并支付
↓
支付网关 webhook 或链上监听确认付款
↓
后端把订单标记为 paid
↓
Godot 轮询到 paid
↓
自动解锁完整版
```

**MVP 功能范围**
第一版只做一个商品：

```text
Product ID: full_game
Name: Full Version
Price: $4.99 或 $9.99
Network: Polygon / BSC 优先
Currency: USDT / USDC / MATIC
Unlock: 全章节 / 完整内容 / 高级收藏系统
```

暂时不做：
- NFT 道具
- 游戏内交易市场
- Play-to-earn
- 钱包登录账号体系
- 多商品商城
- 退款自动化
- 复杂订阅

**系统模块**
```text
Godot Client
- 显示购买入口
- 获取 device_id
- 创建订单
- 打开 checkout_url
- 轮询订单状态
- paid 后解锁内容
- 本地缓存 license

Backend API
- 创建订单
- 查询订单状态
- 接收 webhook
- 验证支付金额/币种/订单号
- 生成 license
- 返回解锁状态

Checkout Page
- 显示商品信息
- 连接钱包
- 发起支付
- 显示支付成功/等待确认

Payment Provider / Chain Listener
- NOWPayments / Coinbase Commerce / 自建链上监听
- 通知后端付款成功
```

**后端接口**
```text
POST /api/orders
输入: device_id, product_id
输出: order_id, checkout_url

GET /api/orders/:order_id
输出: pending | paid | expired | failed

POST /api/payment-webhook
支付成功回调，只允许支付服务调用

GET /api/license/:device_id
查询该设备是否已解锁
```

**订单状态**
```text
pending   已创建，等待付款
paid      已确认付款，可以解锁
expired   超时未付款
failed    付款失败或金额不匹配
refunded  后续版本再支持
```

**安全要求**
- Godot 客户端不能自己声明“已付款”。
- webhook 必须校验签名。
- 后端必须校验金额、币种、订单 ID、收款地址。
- 每个订单只能解锁一次。
- device_id 只用于低成本授权，不要当作强身份。
- 本地 license 可以被破解，所以高价值内容应以后端状态为准。

**推荐第一版技术选型**
```text
Game: Godot
Backend: Node.js / Express
Database: SQLite 起步，后续 PostgreSQL
Payment: NOWPayments 或 Coinbase Commerce
Network: Polygon / BSC
Currency: USDT / USDC
Hosting: Cloudflare Workers / Render / Fly.io / Railway
```

**为什么第一版不用 UAccess 自动解锁**
UAccess 更适合“付款后下载文件”，不适合直接把支付成功状态推给 Godot。它可以作为手动 license code 方案，但如果要自动解锁，最好用自己的 checkout + 后端订单系统。

**上线顺序**
1. 做 Godot 内 `Unlock Full Version` 按钮。
2. 后端实现订单表和 `POST /api/orders`。
3. 接入一个支付网关 checkout。
4. 实现 webhook，把订单改成 `paid`。
5. Godot 每 3 秒查询订单状态。
6. paid 后写入本地 license 并解锁。
7. 上线前做小额真实支付测试。

**产品判断**
这套方案适合：
- itch.io 免费 Demo
- 自己网站/checkout 收加密货币
- PC 完整版解锁
- 小额一次性购买

不适合：
- Steam 版本
- App Store / Google Play 内购替代
- 强监管地区的大规模商业发行
- 高投机 NFT/GameFi 玩法

建议第一版目标很窄：**只做一次性购买完整版自动解锁**。等这条链路跑通，再考虑钱包账号、NFT 收藏品或多商品商城。

[171] user: 产品内部如何完成支付


[172] assistant: 产品内部完成支付，最稳的是 **“内置购买入口 + 外部 Checkout + 后端确认 + 自动解锁”**。游戏里看起来像内购，但真正付款在网页完成。

**产品内流程**
```text
1. 玩家在 Demo 里看到锁定内容
2. 点击 Unlock Full Version
3. 游戏弹出购买页说明
4. 玩家点 Pay with Crypto
5. 游戏打开浏览器 Checkout
6. 玩家用钱包支付
7. 回到游戏等待确认
8. 游戏自动显示 “Unlocked”
9. 完整版内容开放
```

**游戏内页面设计**
```text
Unlock Full Version

Includes:
- All chapters
- Full treasure collection
- Companion rewards
- Challenge levels

Price: $4.99
Pay with crypto

[Unlock Now]
[Restore Purchase]
```

**关键按钮**
- `Unlock Now`：创建订单并打开支付页
- `I have paid`：手动刷新订单状态
- `Restore Purchase`：根据设备 ID / license code / 邮箱恢复购买
- `Enter License Code`：备用方案，防止自动解锁失败

**内部状态**
```text
free_demo
payment_pending
paid_confirming
unlocked
payment_failed
restore_needed
```

**用户体验细节**
支付后不要让玩家猜：

```text
Waiting for payment confirmation...
This usually takes 10-60 seconds.
You can safely return to the game after payment.
```

如果没确认：

```text
Payment not detected yet.
[Check Again]
[Open Payment Page]
[Enter License Code]
```

**产品规则**
- Demo 可玩，不强制钱包登录。
- 购买只解锁完整版，不影响基础体验。
- 不卖战力，不做抽卡，不做代币奖励。
- 价格用美元展示，支付页再显示链和币种。
- 默认推荐 Polygon / BSC，避免 ETH 主网高 gas。

**最小 MVP**
```text
商品：Full Version
价格：$4.99
支付方式：USDT / USDC / MATIC
解锁方式：后端确认 paid 后自动解锁
备用方式：license code 手动输入
```

**真正完成支付的系统链路**
```text
Godot 创建订单
↓
后端生成 checkout_url
↓
玩家浏览器付款
↓
支付服务 webhook 通知后端
↓
后端标记订单 paid
↓
Godot 查询到 paid
↓
本地保存 unlocked = true
```

一句话：  
**游戏内不要直接收钱；游戏内只发起购买、显示状态、解锁内容。支付和验证交给网页 + 后端。**

[173] user: 如果pc版本可以选择钱包登陆，支付解锁用合约方式可以完成吗


[174] assistant: 可以完成，而且 PC 版比移动端更适合做这套：**钱包登录 + 合约支付 + 自动解锁**。

但建议你把它设计成“可选钱包登录”，不要强制玩家一进游戏就连接钱包。

**推荐产品流程**
```text
玩家打开 PC Demo
↓
可直接试玩
↓
点击 Unlock Full Version
↓
选择 Connect Wallet
↓
浏览器打开钱包连接页
↓
玩家用 MetaMask / WalletConnect 登录
↓
调用合约 pay(order_id)
↓
链上交易确认
↓
后端监听合约事件
↓
Godot 查询订单 paid
↓
自动解锁完整版
```

**核心结构**
```text
Godot PC Client
- 显示登录/购买入口
- 打开钱包网页
- 轮询订单状态
- 解锁内容

Wallet Checkout Web Page
- 连接 MetaMask / WalletConnect
- 显示价格
- 调用智能合约支付
- 显示交易状态

Payment Smart Contract
- 接收付款
- 记录 order_id / buyer / amount
- emit PaymentReceived

Backend Listener
- 监听 PaymentReceived 事件
- 校验金额和订单
- 标记 paid
- 生成 license

Godot Unlock
- 查询 paid
- 保存 unlocked
```

**合约支付逻辑**
合约只做简单收款，不要第一版就做复杂 NFT：

```solidity
function pay(bytes32 orderId) external payable {
    require(msg.value == PRICE, "wrong amount");
    require(!paidOrders[orderId], "already paid");

    paidOrders[orderId] = true;

    emit PaymentReceived(orderId, msg.sender, msg.value);
}
```

如果用 USDC / USDT：

```solidity
function pay(bytes32 orderId) external {
    require(!paidOrders[orderId], "already paid");

    token.transferFrom(msg.sender, treasury, PRICE);

    paidOrders[orderId] = true;

    emit PaymentReceived(orderId, msg.sender, PRICE);
}
```

**为什么仍然需要后端**
即使链上有记录，Godot 客户端不应该自己决定已付款。原因：

- 客户端容易被改
- 需要防止错误金额
- 需要处理链重组/失败交易
- 需要做 license 恢复
- 需要给非技术玩家更稳定体验

后端监听合约事件后，把订单状态变成 `paid`，Godot 只相信后端。

**钱包登录怎么做**
推荐用网页完成，不要直接在 Godot 里接钱包 SDK：

```text
Godot 打开:
https://yourgame.com/checkout?order_id=xxx

网页里:
- Connect Wallet
- Sign message 登录
- Pay with USDC
- Done
```

Godot 只拿：
```text
order_id
wallet_address
status: pending / paid
```

**推荐链**
第一版建议：

```text
Polygon + USDC
或
Base + USDC
或
BSC + USDT
```

不建议第一版用 Ethereum 主网，gas 对小额游戏太贵。

**产品上怎么做最好**
```text
Unlock Full Version
$4.99

Options:
[Pay with Wallet]
[Enter License Code]
[Restore Purchase]
```

支付页显示：
```text
Connect Wallet
Pay 4.99 USDC on Polygon
Waiting for confirmation...
Payment confirmed. You can return to the game.
```

**是否可以不用后端，纯合约解锁？**
技术上可以，但不建议。

纯客户端读取合约：
```text
Godot 查询链上 order_id paid
↓
解锁
```

问题是：
- RPC 不稳定
- 多链处理麻烦
- 客户端容易被篡改
- 恢复购买和客服困难
- 不能方便地封禁异常订单
- 离线授权体验差

所以生产方案还是：

```text
合约 = 收款和公开凭证
后端 = 授权和订单真相
Godot = 展示和解锁
```

**最小可行版本**
1. PC Demo 内加 `Unlock Full Version`
2. 后端创建 `order_id`
3. 网页连接钱包
4. Polygon/Base USDC 合约收款
5. 合约 emit `PaymentReceived`
6. 后端监听事件
7. Godot 轮询订单状态
8. paid 后解锁完整版

这套可以完成，并且比 UAccess 更产品化。

---
name: gmail
description: Manage Gmail inbox triage, mailbox search, thread summaries, action extraction, reply drafting, and email forwarding through connected Gmail data. Use when the user wants to inspect a mailbox or thread, search email with Gmail query syntax, summarize messages, extract decisions and follow-ups, prepare replies or forwarded messages, or organize messages with explicit confirmation before send, archive, delete, or label actions.
---

# Gmail

## Overview

Use this skill to turn noisy email threads into clear summaries, action lists, and ready-to-send drafts. Prefer Gmail-native search and read workflows, preserve message context, and avoid changing message state without explicit user intent.

## Preferred Deliverables

- Thread briefs that capture the latest status, decisions, open questions, and next actions.
- Reply or forward drafts that are ready to paste, review, or send.
- Inbox triage lists that group messages by urgency or follow-up state.

## Workflow Skills

| Workflow | Skill |
| --- | --- |
| Inbox triage, urgency ranking, and follow-up detection | [../gmail-inbox-triage/SKILL.md](../gmail-inbox-triage/SKILL.md) |

## Reference Notes

| Task | Reference |
| --- | --- |
| Search planning, refinement, pagination, and body-fetch strategy | [references/search-workflow.md](./references/search-workflow.md) |
| Label application, relabeling, and label-based cleanup | [references/label-actions.md](./references/label-actions.md) |
| Reply drafting, reply-all decisions, and tone matching | [references/reply-workflow.md](./references/reply-workflow.md) |
| Email forwarding, context notes, and intent framing | [references/forward-workflow.md](./references/forward-workflow.md) |

## Mailbox Analysis Pattern

For mailbox analysis requests such as triage, follow-up detection, topic summaries, cleanup, thread understandi<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="607" />rief confidence note when necessary.

## Output Conventions

- Summaries should lead with the latest status, then list decisions, open questions, and action items.
- Inbox triage should use explicit buckets such as urgent, waiting, and FYI when that helps the user scan quickly.
- When ranking urgency or follow-up state, state the search scope and coverage, such as "from the most recent 15 inbox messages" or "from unread inbox messages matching this query."
- When the task depends on whether the user "opened" or ignored email, treat that as an inference from Gmail read state unless the connector exposes stronger engagement data.
- Avoid absolute claims like "the only urgent email" unless the mailbox scan was comprehensive enough to support that conclusion.
- When the result comes from a narrowed search or shortlist, report that confidence and mention what was excluded.
- Draft replies should be concise and ready to paste or send, with greeting, body, and closing when appropriate.
- If a reply depends on missing facts, present a short draft plus a list of unresolved details.
- When multiple emails are involved, reference the sender and timestamp of the message that matters most.
- Avoid repetitive meta-explanations about inaccessible internal sources in normal deliverables. If the user wants provenance, summarize the evidence used; otherwise keep the output focused on the draft, summary, or next action.

## Example Requests

- "Summarize the latest thread with Acme and tell me what I still owe them."
- "Draft a reply that confirms Tuesday works and asks for the final agenda."
- "Go through my unread inbox and group emails into urgent, waiting, and low priority."
- "Prepare a polite follow-up to the recruiter thread if I have not replied yet."

## Light Fallback

If thread or inbox data is missing, say that Gmail access may be unavailable or scoped to the wrong account and ask the user to reconnect or clarify which mailbox or thread should be used.

---
name: gmail
description: Manage Gmail inbox triage, mailbox search, thread summaries, action extraction, reply drafting, and email forwarding through connected Gmail data. Use when the user wants to inspect a mailbox or thread, search email with Gmail query syntax, summarize messages, extract decisions and follow-ups, prepare replies or forwarded messages, or organize messages with explicit confirmation before send, archive, delete, or label actions.
---

# Gmail

## Overview

Use this skill to turn noisy email threads into clear summaries, action lists, and ready-to-send drafts. Prefer Gmail-native search and read workflows, preserve message context, and avoid changing message state without explicit user intent.

## Preferred Deliverables

- Thread briefs that capture the latest status, decisions, open questions, and next actions.
- Reply or forward drafts that are ready to paste, review, or send.
- Inbox triage lists that group messages by urgency or follow-up state.

## Workflow Skills

| Workflow | Skill |
| --- | --- |
| Inbox triage, urgency ranking, and follow-up detection | [../gmail-inbox-triage/SKILL.md](../gmail-inbox-triage/SKILL.md) |

## Reference Notes

| Task | Reference |
| --- | --- |
| Search planning, refinement, pagination, and body-fetch strategy | [references/search-workflow.md](./references/search-workflow.md) |
| Label application, relabeling, and label-based cleanup | [references/label-actions.md](./references/label-actions.md) |
| Reply drafting, reply-all decisions, and tone matching | [references/reply-workflow.md](./references/reply-workflow.md) |
| Email forwarding, context notes, and intent framing | [references/forward-workflow.md](./references/forward-workflow.md) |

## Mailbox Analysis Pattern

For mailbox analysis requests such as triage, follow-up detection, topic summaries, cleanup, thread understandi<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="607" />rief confidence note when necessary.

## Output Conventions

- Summaries should lead with the latest status, then list decisions, open questions, and action items.
- Inbox triage should use explicit buckets such as urgent, waiting, and FYI when that helps the user scan quickly.
- When ranking urgency or follow-up state, state the search scope and coverage, such as "from the most recent 15 inbox messages" or "from unread inbox messages matching this query."
- When the task depends on whether the user "opened" or ignored email, treat that as an inference from Gmail read state unless the connector exposes stronger engagement data.
- Avoid absolute claims like "the only urgent email" unless the mailbox scan was comprehensive enough to support that conclusion.
- When the result comes from a narrowed search or shortlist, report that confidence and mention what was excluded.
- Draft replies should be concise and ready to paste or send, with greeting, body, and closing when appropriate.
- If a reply depends on missing facts, present a short draft plus a list of unresolved details.
- When multiple emails are involved, reference the sender and timestamp of the message that matters most.
- Avoid repetitive meta-explanations about inaccessible internal sources in normal deliverables. If the user wants provenance, summarize the evidence used; otherwise keep the output focused on the draft, summary, or next action.

## Example Requests

- "Summarize the latest thread with Acme and tell me what I still owe them."
- "Draft a reply that confirms Tuesday works and asks for the final agenda."
- "Go through my unread inbox and group emails into urgent, waiting, and low priority."
- "Prepare a polite follow-up to the recruiter thread if I have not replied yet."

## Light Fallback

If thread or inbox data is missing, say that Gmail access may be unavailable or scoped to the wrong account and ask the user to reconnect or clarify which mailbox or thread should be used.

[217] assistant: 其他线程能发，主要是因为它们的 **执行环境/权限状态和当前交互线程不一样**。

我看到以前的记录里，`Hourly AI video editing tools scan` 这类自动化曾经通过 Gmail `_send_email` 成功发送，并拿到过 message id，例如 `19e4ad54d463638c`。这说明 Gmail 连接器本身曾经是可用的。

这次失败不是 Gmail 插件不存在，也不是收件人错误，而是当前线程里调用 `_send_email` 时，系统的 **automatic permission approval review** 连续超时。也就是说：发送动作没有被拒绝，只是审批流程没在 90 秒内返回。

差异大概率来自：

- 旧线程/自动化已经在当时完成了 Gmail 授权和审批链路。
- cron/heartbeat 自动化可能在后台执行，不走当前这次交互式发送的同一个审批等待路径。
- 当前线程直接发送外部邮件时，仍需要动作级审批；审批服务这次超时。
- 创建草稿不需要同等级别的发送审批，所以 `_create_draft` 成功了。

所以结论是：**不是“其他线程可以、这个线程永远不可以”，而是当前 Gmail 直接发送动作卡在权限审批超时。**

可行处理：

1. 先手动发送我创建的草稿，确认邮箱链路。
2. 等下一轮 `Hourly game AI industry scan` 自动化实际运行，看它是否能后台直接发送。
3. 如果自动化也失败，再说明 Gmail 发送权限/连接器需要重新授权。
4. 如果只当前线程失败，以后可以用“创建草稿 + 手动发送”作为备用。

---
name: gmail
description: Manage Gmail inbox triage, mailbox search, thread summaries, action extraction, reply drafting, and email forwarding through connected Gmail data. Use when the user wants to inspect a mailbox or thread, search email with Gmail query syntax, summarize messages, extract decisions and follow-ups, prepare replies or forwarded messages, or organize messages with explicit confirmation before send, archive, delete, or label actions.
---

# Gmail

## Overview

Use this skill to turn noisy email threads into clear summaries, action lists, and ready-to-send drafts. Prefer Gmail-native search and read workflows, preserve message context, and avoid changing message state without explicit user intent.

## Preferred Deliverables

- Thread briefs that capture the latest status, decisions, open questions, and next actions.
- Reply or forward drafts that are ready to paste, review, or send.
- Inbox triage lists that group messages by urgency or follow-up state.

## Workflow Skills

| Workflow | Skill |
| --- | --- |
| Inbox triage, urgency ranking, and follow-up detection | [../gmail-inbox-triage/SKILL.md](../gmail-inbox-triage/SKILL.md) |

## Reference Notes

| Task | Reference |
| --- | --- |
| Search planning, refinement, pagination, and body-fetch strategy | [references/search-workflow.md](./references/search-workflow.md) |
| Label application, relabeling, and label-based cleanup | [references/label-actions.md](./references/label-actions.md) |
| Reply drafting, reply-all decisions, and tone matching | [references/reply-workflow.md](./references/reply-workflow.md) |
| Email forwarding, context notes, and intent framing | [references/forward-workflow.md](./references/forward-workflow.md) |

## Mailbox Analysis Pattern

For mailbox analysis requests such as triage, follow-up detection, topic summaries, cleanup, thread understandi<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="607" />rief confidence note when necessary.

## Output Conventions

- Summaries should lead with the latest status, then list decisions, open questions, and action items.
- Inbox triage should use explicit buckets such as urgent, waiting, and FYI when that helps the user scan quickly.
- When ranking urgency or follow-up state, state the search scope and coverage, such as "from the most recent 15 inbox messages" or "from unread inbox messages matching this query."
- When the task depends on whether the user "opened" or ignored email, treat that as an inference from Gmail read state unless the connector exposes stronger engagement data.
- Avoid absolute claims like "the only urgent email" unless the mailbox scan was comprehensive enough to support that conclusion.
- When the result comes from a narrowed search or shortlist, report that confidence and mention what was excluded.
- Draft replies should be concise and ready to paste or send, with greeting, body, and closing when appropriate.
- If a reply depends on missing facts, present a short draft plus a list of unresolved details.
- When multiple emails are involved, reference the sender and timestamp of the message that matters most.
- Avoid repetitive meta-explanations about inaccessible internal sources in normal deliverables. If the user wants provenance, summarize the evidence used; otherwise keep the output focused on the draft, summary, or next action.

## Example Requests

- "Summarize the latest thread with Acme and tell me what I still owe them."
- "Draft a reply that confirms Tuesday works and asks for the final agenda."
- "Go through my unread inbox and group emails into urgent, waiting, and low priority."
- "Prepare a polite follow-up to the recruiter thread if I have not replied yet."

## Light Fallback

If thread or inbox data is missing, say that Gmail access may be unavailable or scoped to the wrong account and ask the user to reconnect or clarify which mailbox or thread should be used.

[217] assistant: 其他线程能发，主要是因为它们的 **执行环境/权限状态和当前交互线程不一样**。

我看到以前的记录里，`Hourly AI video editing tools scan` 这类自动化曾经通过 Gmail `_send_email` 成功发送，并拿到过 message id，例如 `19e4ad54d463638c`。这说明 Gmail 连接器本身曾经是可用的。

这次失败不是 Gmail 插件不存在，也不是收件人错误，而是当前线程里调用 `_send_email` 时，系统的 **automatic permission approval review** 连续超时。也就是说：发送动作没有被拒绝，只是审批流程没在 90 秒内返回。

差异大概率来自：

- 旧线程/自动化已经在当时完成了 Gmail 授权和审批链路。
- cron/heartbeat 自动化可能在后台执行，不走当前这次交互式发送的同一个审批等待路径。
- 当前线程直接发送外部邮件时，仍需要动作级审批；审批服务这次超时。
- 创建草稿不需要同等级别的发送审批，所以 `_create_draft` 成功了。

所以结论是：**不是“其他线程可以、这个线程永远不可以”，而是当前 Gmail 直接发送动作卡在权限审批超时。**

可行处理：

1. 先手动发送我创建的草稿，确认邮箱链路。
2. 等下一轮 `Hourly game AI industry scan` 自动化实际运行，看它是否能后台直接发送。
3. 如果自动化也失败，再说明 Gmail 发送权限/连接器需要重新授权。
4. 如果只当前线程失败，以后可以用“创建草稿 + 手动发送”作为备用。

---
name: gmail
description: Manage Gmail inbox triage, mailbox search, thread summaries, action extraction, reply drafting, and email forwarding through connected Gmail data. Use when the user wants to inspect a mailbox or thread, search email with Gmail query syntax, summarize messages, extract decisions and follow-ups, prepare replies or forwarded messages, or organize messages with explicit confirmation before send, archive, delete, or label actions.
---

# Gmail

## Overview

Use this skill to turn noisy email threads into clear summaries, action lists, and ready-to-send drafts. Prefer Gmail-native search and read workflows, preserve message context, and avoid changing message state without explicit user intent.

## Preferred Deliverables

- Thread briefs that capture the latest status, decisions, open questions, and next actions.
- Reply or forward drafts that are ready to paste, review, or send.
- Inbox triage lists that group messages by urgency or follow-up state.

## Workflow Skills

| Workflow | Skill |
| --- | --- |
| Inbox triage, urgency ranking, and follow-up detection | [../gmail-inbox-triage/SKILL.md](../gmail-inbox-triage/SKILL.md) |

## Reference Notes

| Task | Reference |
| --- | --- |
| Search planning, refinement, pagination, and body-fetch strategy | [references/search-workflow.md](./references/search-workflow.md) |
| Label application, relabeling, and label-based cleanup | [references/label-actions.md](./references/label-actions.md) |
| Reply drafting, reply-all decisions, and tone matching | [references/reply-workflow.md](./references/reply-workflow.md) |
| Email forwarding, context notes, and intent framing | [references/forward-workflow.md](./references/forward-workflow.md) |

## Mailbox Analysis Pattern

For mailbox analysis requests such as triage, follow-up detection, topic summaries, cleanup, thread understandi<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="607" />rief confidence note when necessary.

## Output Conventions

- Summaries should lead with the latest status, then list decisions, open questions, and action items.
- Inbox triage should use explicit buckets such as urgent, waiting, and FYI when that helps the user scan quickly.
- When ranking urgency or follow-up state, state the search scope and coverage, such as "from the most recent 15 inbox messages" or "from unread inbox messages matching this query."
- When the task depends on whether the user "opened" or ignored email, treat that as an inference from Gmail read state unless the connector exposes stronger engagement data.
- Avoid absolute claims like "the only urgent email" unless the mailbox scan was comprehensive enough to support that conclusion.
- When the result comes from a narrowed search or shortlist, report that confidence and mention what was excluded.
- Draft replies should be concise and ready to paste or send, with greeting, body, and closing when appropriate.
- If a reply depends on missing facts, present a short draft plus a list of unresolved details.
- When multiple emails are involved, reference the sender and timestamp of the message that matters most.
- Avoid repetitive meta-explanations about inaccessible internal sources in normal deliverables. If the user wants provenance, summarize the evidence used; otherwise keep the output focused on the draft, summary, or next action.

## Example Requests

- "Summarize the latest thread with Acme and tell me what I still owe them."
- "Draft a reply that confirms Tuesday works and asks for the final agenda."
- "Go through my unread inbox and group emails into urgent, waiting, and low priority."
- "Prepare a polite follow-up to the recruiter thread if I have not replied yet."

## Light Fallback

If thread or inbox data is missing, say that Gmail access may be unavailable or scoped to the wrong account and ask the user to reconnect or clarify which mailbox or thread should be used.

[217] assistant: 其他线程能发，主要是因为它们的 **执行环境/权限状态和当前交互线程不一样**。

我看到以前的记录里，`Hourly AI video editing tools scan` 这类自动化曾经通过 Gmail `_send_email` 成功发送，并拿到过 message id，例如 `19e4ad54d463638c`。这说明 Gmail 连接器本身曾经是可用的。

这次失败不是 Gmail 插件不存在，也不是收件人错误，而是当前线程里调用 `_send_email` 时，系统的 **automatic permission approval review** 连续超时。也就是说：发送动作没有被拒绝，只是审批流程没在 90 秒内返回。

差异大概率来自：

- 旧线程/自动化已经在当时完成了 Gmail 授权和审批链路。
- cron/heartbeat 自动化可能在后台执行，不走当前这次交互式发送的同一个审批等待路径。
- 当前线程直接发送外部邮件时，仍需要动作级审批；审批服务这次超时。
- 创建草稿不需要同等级别的发送审批，所以 `_create_draft` 成功了。

所以结论是：**不是“其他线程可以、这个线程永远不可以”，而是当前 Gmail 直接发送动作卡在权限审批超时。**

可行处理：

1. 先手动发送我创建的草稿，确认邮箱链路。
2. 等下一轮 `Hourly game AI industry scan` 自动化实际运行，看它是否能后台直接发送。
3. 如果自动化也失败，再说明 Gmail 发送权限/连接器需要重新授权。
4. 如果只当前线程失败，以后可以用“创建草稿 + 手动发送”作为备用。

---
name: gmail
description: Manage Gmail inbox triage, mailbox search, thread summaries, action extraction, reply drafting, and email forwarding through connected Gmail data. Use when the user wants to inspect a mailbox or thread, search email with Gmail query syntax, summarize messages, extract decisions and follow-ups, prepare replies or forwarded messages, or organize messages with explicit confirmation before send, archive, delete, or label actions.
---

# Gmail

## Overview

Use this skill to turn noisy email threads into clear summaries, action lists, and ready-to-send drafts. Prefer Gmail-native search and read workflows, preserve message context, and avoid changing message state without explicit user intent.

## Preferred Deliverables

- Thread briefs that capture the latest status, decisions, open questions, and next actions.
- Reply or forward drafts that are ready to paste, review, or send.
- Inbox triage lists that group messages by urgency or follow-up state.

## Workflow Skills

| Workflow | Skill |
| --- | --- |
| Inbox triage, urgency ranking, and follow-up detection | [../gmail-inbox-triage/SKILL.md](../gmail-inbox-triage/SKILL.md) |

## Reference Notes

| Task | Reference |
| --- | --- |
| Search planning, refinement, pagination, and body-fetch strategy | [references/search-workflow.md](./references/search-workflow.md) |
| Label application, relabeling, and label-based cleanup | [references/label-actions.md](./references/label-actions.md) |
| Reply drafting, reply-all decisions, and tone matching | [references/reply-workflow.md](./references/reply-workflow.md) |
| Email forwarding, context notes, and intent framing | [references/forward-workflow.md](./references/forward-workflow.md) |

## Mailbox Analysis Pattern

For mailbox analysis requests such as triage, follow-up detection, topic summaries, cleanup, thread understandi<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="607" />rief confidence note when necessary.

## Output Conventions

- Summaries should lead with the latest status, then list decisions, open questions, and action items.
- Inbox triage should use explicit buckets such as urgent, waiting, and FYI when that helps the user scan quickly.
- When ranking urgency or follow-up state, state the search scope and coverage, such as "from the most recent 15 inbox messages" or "from unread inbox messages matching this query."
- When the task depends on whether the user "opened" or ignored email, treat that as an inference from Gmail read state unless the connector exposes stronger engagement data.
- Avoid absolute claims like "the only urgent email" unless the mailbox scan was comprehensive enough to support that conclusion.
- When the result comes from a narrowed search or shortlist, report that confidence and mention what was excluded.
- Draft replies should be concise and ready to paste or send, with greeting, body, and closing when appropriate.
- If a reply depends on missing facts, present a short draft plus a list of unresolved details.
- When multiple emails are involved, reference the sender and timestamp of the message that matters most.
- Avoid repetitive meta-explanations about inaccessible internal sources in normal deliverables. If the user wants provenance, summarize the evidence used; otherwise keep the output focused on the draft, summary, or next action.

## Example Requests

- "Summarize the latest thread with Acme and tell me what I still owe them."
- "Draft a reply that confirms Tuesday works and asks for the final agenda."
- "Go through my unread inbox and group emails into urgent, waiting, and low priority."
- "Prepare a polite follow-up to the recruiter thread if I have not replied yet."

## Light Fallback

If thread or inbox data is missing, say that Gmail access may be unavailable or scoped to the wrong account and ask the user to reconnect or clarify which mailbox or thread should be used.

[217] assistant: 其他线程能发，主要是因为它们的 **执行环境/权限状态和当前交互线程不一样**。

我看到以前的记录里，`Hourly AI video editing tools scan` 这类自动化曾经通过 Gmail `_send_email` 成功发送，并拿到过 message id，例如 `19e4ad54d463638c`。这说明 Gmail 连接器本身曾经是可用的。

这次失败不是 Gmail 插件不存在，也不是收件人错误，而是当前线程里调用 `_send_email` 时，系统的 **automatic permission approval review** 连续超时。也就是说：发送动作没有被拒绝，只是审批流程没在 90 秒内返回。

差异大概率来自：

- 旧线程/自动化已经在当时完成了 Gmail 授权和审批链路。
- cron/heartbeat 自动化可能在后台执行，不走当前这次交互式发送的同一个审批等待路径。
- 当前线程直接发送外部邮件时，仍需要动作级审批；审批服务这次超时。
- 创建草稿不需要同等级别的发送审批，所以 `_create_draft` 成功了。

所以结论是：**不是“其他线程可以、这个线程永远不可以”，而是当前 Gmail 直接发送动作卡在权限审批超时。**

可行处理：

1. 先手动发送我创建的草稿，确认邮箱链路。
2. 等下一轮 `Hourly game AI industry scan` 自动化实际运行，看它是否能后台直接发送。
3. 如果自动化也失败，再说明 Gmail 发送权限/连接器需要重新授权。
4. 如果只当前线程失败，以后可以用“创建草稿 + 手动发送”作为备用。
